Septimana philosophica (Michael Maier, 1620)
Public domain. Sourced from Internet Archive: the 1620 Frankfurt Latin edition (Aenigmata aureola de omni naturae genere) (https://archive.org/details/septimanaphiloso00maie). Maier's philosophical week: golden enigmas on every kind of nature, framed as answers of Solomon and the Queen of Sheba — alchemy, nature-philosophy and emblematic wisdom in Latin.
Hosted by the Tower of Babel library, antarctic-labs.com.
